# Recorded results inventory, 22 September 2026

Latest supplement (23 September): all sixteen prospective dense-maze runs have completed.
Verified round trips: action-conditioned 1/4, command history 3/4, reactive 0/4,
no future action 0/4; zero contacts and pipeline faults across all sixteen.
The original 1,036-record
snapshot below and its CSV remain unchanged. Completed results added since that
snapshot, including nested diagnostics omitted by its discovery rule, are:

| Completed study | Result | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Command-history coverage of the proposed maze predictor-training data | 2448 contexts contain 153 distinct past-plus-700-ms-future command sequences; none is entirely stationary, whereas 951/1197 control and 938/1197 treatment navigation windows are. Only 6/1197 and 8/1197 combined sequences match the new tape. The original training pool does contain 480 stationary sequences; this is a gap in the maze supplement, not evidence of zero stationary training. No new fit | [Coverage analysis and limits](go2_maze_view_readout_navigation_2026-09-23.md) |
| Exposed layout 00, maze-data readout treatment; first matched navigation pair complete | Both control and treatment have no goal/home arrivals at 480.32 s, zero contacts/faults. Treatment: 979/1198 holds, longest zero request 274.40 s; translation learned/command-history error 79.65/9.01 mm on its 95 executed windows. Different trajectories prohibit causal interpretation of cross-run RMSE. Corrected prefix analysis uses scan scores for view-seeking decisions; original outputs retained. Layout-02 pair pending | [Paired navigation outcomes, diagnostics and correction](go2_maze_view_readout_navigation_2026-09-23.md) |
| Shared-command-prefix errors on the completed exposed layout-00 readout control | 1197 executed windows, 100/200/300/700-ms evaluation; existing 700-ms metrics reproduced. At 300 ms, learned/command-history XY RMSE is 25.18/6.11 mm on 60 translation windows and 3.47/1.70 mm on 896 reserve-limited holds with a zero requested prefix. Post-hoc overlapping-window diagnosis, no navigation intervention | [Prefix results and limitations](go2_maze_view_readout_navigation_2026-09-23.md) |
| Exposed layout 00, matched existing-data readout navigation control | No goal/home arrivals; 480.32-s budget exhausted; zero contacts/faults. 987/1198 hold plans, longest zero-request interval 358.82 s. At 700 ms, translation XY RMSE learned/command-history 66.94/13.59 mm over 60 overlapping windows. Paired maze-data treatment also completed without an arrival; layout-02 pair remains pending | [Study results and decision diagnosis](go2_maze_view_readout_navigation_2026-09-23.md) |
| Predictor/readout decomposition on the two maze-view evaluation layouts | All 240 fixed rows complete. With the maze-data readout, 700-ms action translation error is 46.62/52.15 mm versus no-future-action 78.65/81.22, persistence 103.42/103.96, observed future 27.02/27.17 and command history 9.92/9.68. Actual-future outputs reproduced exactly; no navigation or JEPA-objective claim | [Full decomposition and limits](go2_maze_view_prospective_transfer_2026-09-23.md) |
| Matched existing-data versus maze-data readout fitting and original maze diagnostic | Both 440-update fits and all 128 evaluation rows complete. At 700 ms, maze-data action translation XY RMSE is 64.82 mm versus initialization 67.17, existing-data control 73.33 and command history 8.65; turn action yaw improves 10.24 to 6.95 degrees. No navigation promotion | [Full diagnostic and checkpoint identities](go2_maze_view_training_2026-09-22.md) |
| Actual-future decoder transfer on two separately prepared new mazes | All 240 rows complete. At 700 ms, translation XY RMSE initial/control/maze-data is 91.60/94.99/27.02 mm on maze 0 and 94.91/98.81/27.17 mm on maze 1; command history remains stronger at 9.92/9.68 mm. Same fixed-tape distribution; no predictive-planning or navigation claim | [Transfer tables and limits](go2_maze_view_prospective_transfer_2026-09-23.md) |
| Frozen command references on the two new evaluation mazes | All 240 fixed windows complete before readout-fit results. At 700 ms, translation-departure command-history XY RMSE is 9.92 / 9.68 mm for mazes 0 / 1, versus integrated commands 28.64 / 28.81 mm and zero motion 108.18 / 108.12 mm. All predictions match the runtime command implementation within 5.56e-17 | [Transfer references and limits](go2_maze_view_prospective_transfer_2026-09-23.md) |
| Prospective evaluation-only maze-view collection | Eight recordings complete on two new mazes excluded from the explicit 113-layout registry; 1368 camera pairs, zero contacts/stops. Fixed 240 evaluation rows and 360 image paths before fit completion; completed decoder evaluation reported above | [Prospective transfer design and collection](go2_maze_view_prospective_transfer_2026-09-23.md) |
| Complete-cohort same-window motion comparison | Command-history translation error is lower on all 12 trajectories containing translation windows; neural/command-history RMSE ratios 4.63--15.03. Four stationary blind runs have no translation windows. Descriptive, overlapping-window evidence, not causal navigation attribution | [Per-trajectory table](go2_dense_world_model_maze_cohort_2026-09-18.md), [all sixteen records](go2_dense_world_model_maze_motion_comparison_2026-09-23.json) |
| Prospective layout 03, command history; cohort complete | Physically verified round trip in 234.02 simulated seconds; zero contacts/faults; all nine return edges reversed outbound edges. Final cohort: action 1/4, command history 3/4, reactive 0/4, no future action 0/4 | [Completed cohort](go2_dense_world_model_maze_cohort_2026-09-18.md), [all sixteen assignments](go2_dense_world_model_maze_cohort_complete_result_2026-09-23.json) |
| Prospective layout 03, no future action | No arrivals; 480.32-s budget exhausted; all 1198 plans hold and all 24016 requests zero; zero contacts/faults. Blind cohort complete at 0/4 round trips | [Cohort results](go2_dense_world_model_maze_cohort_2026-09-18.md) |
| Prospective layout 03, action-conditioned | No arrivals; 480.32-s budget exhausted; zero contacts/faults; 971 holds and a 379.62-s longest zero-request interval. Full action-conditioned cohort is 1/4 successful round trips | [Cohort results](go2_dense_world_model_maze_cohort_2026-09-18.md) |

| Prospective layout 02, command history | Physically verified goal/home round trip in 330.92 simulated seconds; zero contacts/faults; all eight return corridor edges reversed observed outbound edges | [Cohort and backtracking results](go2_dense_world_model_maze_cohort_2026-09-18.md) |

Multi-horizon readout fitting and its fixed transfer comparison are complete.
The rows above are a supplement, not an updated count of independent experiments.

Snapshot of 1,036 saved top-level result/failure records referenced by repository reports dated 7–22 September. This is an evidence inventory, not 1,036 independent scientific experiments: it includes predecessor references, diagnostics, infrastructure checks and repeated analyses. Exact-directory-name discovery omits nested-only results and names generated only in code. The companion CSV preserves source-report links and recorded summaries.

Process completion is not scientific success. Native arrival results below come from saved physical readers; blank fields mean unavailable or not applicable. Different controller generations, layouts, repetitions and reused baselines must not be pooled as one success rate. Simulation remains distinct from hardware qualification.

## Records with saved physical arrival evaluation

395 records have saved physical readers. These include short pilots as well as full missions; failed or incomplete missions remain included.
